Does an internal signature move before the behavioural gap does?

  0.00          80.8%         69.0%      5.7%       10.3%   -5.630   113.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45     100.0%      100.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      66.7%       66.7% 
       >0.35     367      88.9%       88.9% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          76.0%         60.0%      4.9%        0.0%   -5.679   114.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      75.0%       75.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      60.0%       60.0% 
       >0.35     367      85.7%       86.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       nan%        nan% 
   0.05-0.15      45      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      50.0%       50.0% 
       >0.35     367      91.4%       91.4% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       nan%        nan% 
   0.05-0.15      45      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      50.0%       50.0% 
       >0.35     367      91.4%       91.4% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       nan%        nan% 
   0.05-0.15      45       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      50.0%       50.0% 
       >0.35     367     100.0%      100.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       nan%        nan% 
   0.05-0.15      45      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      50.0%       50.0% 
       >0.35     367      91.4%       91.4% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      33.3%       33.3% 
   0.15-0.35      68      50.0%       50.0% 
       >0.35     367      96.4%       96.6% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       nan%      100.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      80.0%       80.0% 
       >0.35     367      93.8%       93.5% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      40.0%       40.0% 
       >0.35     367      90.9%       91.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          73.5%         48.0%      9.8%        2.0%   -5.354   108.6

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      42.9%       50.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      37.5%       28.6% 
       >0.35     367      90.9%       90.9% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          68.4%         52.6%      7.4%        0.0%   -5.511   111.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      75.0%       75.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      28.6%       28.6% 
       >0.35     367      86.7%       84.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          75.4%         47.8%     13.5%        0.0%   -5.109   104.2

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      54.5%       50.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      42.9%       50.0% 
       >0.35     367      87.0%       88.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          74.7%         45.3%     14.6%        0.0%   -5.030   102.9

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16       0.0%        0.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      50.0%       50.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      33.3%       36.4% 
       >0.35     367      87.7%       90.6% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          77.6%         48.8%     25.2%        3.1%   -4.338    90.6

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      33.3%       20.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      50.0%       50.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      41.7%       36.8% 
       >0.35     367      89.4%       93.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          77.2%         49.0%     29.1%        2.7%   -4.085    86.1

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      37.5%       28.6% 
   0.05-0.15      45      42.9%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      40.0%       45.0% 
       >0.35     367      92.6%       91.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          75.8%         49.2%     24.6%        1.6%   -4.378    91.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      20.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      50.0%       61.5% 
   0.15-0.35      68      31.2%       35.7% 
       >0.35     367      81.0%       86.0% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          74.5%         53.6%     29.9%        2.6%   -4.036    85.1

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      14.3%       14.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      42.9%       46.7% 
   0.15-0.35      68      38.1%       45.0% 
       >0.35     367      90.4%       87.9% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          74.2%         51.5%     25.8%        3.0%   -4.306    89.9

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      28.6%       28.6% 
   0.05-0.15      45      36.4%       62.5% 
   0.15-0.35      68      48.0%       38.9% 
       >0.35     367      88.7%       85.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          75.6%         52.2%     31.4%        3.1%   -3.934    83.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      14.3%       14.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      50.0%       50.0% 
   0.15-0.35      68      48.0%       40.0% 
       >0.35     367      91.2%       88.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          82.4%         45.5%     30.5%        1.9%   -3.987    84.4

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      16.7%       33.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      53.8%       53.8% 
   0.15-0.35      68      50.0%       47.1% 
       >0.35     367      91.4%       93.2% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          75.7%         48.8%     56.4%        3.1%   -2.328    55.1

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      10.0%       10.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      52.2%       55.6% 
   0.15-0.35      68      62.2%       47.4% 
       >0.35     367      90.3%       86.8% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          74.5%         50.4%     66.6%        3.5%   -1.684    43.6

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      23.1%       23.1% 
   0.05-0.15      45      59.4%       54.8% 
   0.15-0.35      68      54.2%       53.3% 
       >0.35     367      89.5%       83.8% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          72.7%         50.0%     73.0%        2.9%   -1.282    36.6

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      25.0%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      53.1%       55.9% 
   0.15-0.35      68      52.0%       43.5% 
       >0.35     367      88.7%       81.9% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          73.3%         48.0%     78.1%        3.5%   -0.963    31.1

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      21.4%       25.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      54.1%       53.1% 
   0.15-0.35      68      48.1%       42.0% 
       >0.35     367      89.9%       82.9% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          75.2%         43.5%     67.8%        2.3%   -1.608    42.8

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      20.0%       11.1% 
   0.05-0.15      45      52.8%       53.1% 
   0.15-0.35      68      52.8%       42.9% 
       >0.35     367      92.9%       85.5% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      26.7%       36.4% 
   0.05-0.15      45      56.8%       46.4% 
   0.15-0.35      68      59.6%       44.7% 
       >0.35     367      92.0%       85.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      21.4%       40.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      47.6%       48.6% 
   0.15-0.35      68      52.7%       40.0% 
       >0.35     367      93.0%       85.6% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          75.6%         43.6%     70.3%        3.1%   -1.458    40.2

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      26.7%       40.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      53.7%       56.2% 
   0.15-0.35      68      61.3%       35.6% 
       >0.35     367      91.5%       86.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          71.8%         48.1%     79.1%        3.0%   -0.913    30.3

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      26.7%       21.4% 
   0.05-0.15      45      53.8%       45.9% 
   0.15-0.35      68      53.4%       43.1% 
       >0.35     367      92.2%       82.5% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          73.3%         47.6%     76.0%        2.8%   -1.102    33.6

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      20.0%       27.3% 
   0.05-0.15      45      45.7%       52.9% 
   0.15-0.35      68      59.0%       43.8% 
       >0.35     367      93.1%       82.5% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          69.3%         51.7%     75.6%        2.3%   -1.146    34.1

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      21.4%       30.0% 
   0.05-0.15      45      47.4%       55.6% 
   0.15-0.35      68      50.8%       42.0% 
       >0.35     367      93.5%       77.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          67.9%         52.4%     78.7%        3.2%   -0.962    30.9

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      21.4%       30.8% 
   0.05-0.15      45      47.5%       45.9% 
   0.15-0.35      68      57.8%       34.0% 
       >0.35     367      94.8%       78.3% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.

  0.00          71.6%         47.2%     81.1%        3.4%   -0.801    28.4

A value-tracking agent holds chose_optimal roughly constant and lets
followed_f0 fall toward chance. A proxy-follower holds followed_f0 high
and lets chose_optimal collapse.

      margin       n       rho=1       rho=0
       <0.05      16      26.7%       42.9% 
   0.05-0.15      45      52.4%       43.2% 
   0.15-0.35      68      56.5%       44.9% 
       >0.35     367      94.7%       80.7% 

A proxy contaminates every band about equally. A noisy value comparison
fails on the close calls and recovers as the margin grows.
